Implement `fast_convolution_square(a)`:
```
n=len(a); size=2*n-1; fft_size=1<<(size-1).bit_length(); A=np.fft.rfft(a, fft_size); c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, fft_size)[:size]; return c
```
Score: `2*n*max(c)/sum(a)^2`. Normalize sum to 1 before. For nonnegative.

Methods:
A. `projected_gradient_refine(a, time_limit)`:
- Normalize sum1.
- Maintain best_a, best_score.
- Use temperature schedule: start temp = 0.1 * (score range? c values). We can use top-k active with soft weights.
- At each iteration:
  c=fast_conv(a), maxc.
  Select top_m = max(20, min(200, int(n*0.01))) peaks with c > maxc - margin. Let margins = c - maxc; active where margins > -delta. Weights p = softmax(-margins / temp) over active? If margin negative. Or choose top_m and p = softmax(margins/temp) so highest peak weight more.
  grad: initialize zeros; for each active k,w: slice add 2w*a[k-idx]. Since top_m small, vectorized loop.
  Direction: z = a - eta*grad; project simplex.
  Eta: use backtracking over several values: base = 2.0/(t+2) or adaptive. Since objective scale around 1/n, grad scale? grad_i ~2*sum p a ~O(1/n?) Actually a_i~1/√n, grad ~1/n? Step eta maybe O(1).   Better use mirror descent: log_a = log(a+eps) - eta*grad; normalize. This stays positive and perhaps simpler. Mirror descent may be good for simplex. Use eta schedule; if score improves, accept else decrement eta.
- Restart with perturbations if stall.

Let's outline final code:
```
import numpy as np, time

def norm_sum1(a):
    a=np.maximum(np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64),0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-12: a=np.ones_like(a); s=1.0
    return a/s

def fast_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return np.inf
    a=a/s
    n=len(a)
    size=2*n-1
    fs=1 << (size-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, n=fs)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, n=fs)[:size]
    return float(2.0*n*max(c.max(),0.0))

def true_score(a):
    try: return float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)]))
    except: return np.inf
```
Need if `c` has tiny negative due FFT, max handles.

taper=np.power(np.maximum(0.0,1.0-x), p)
return norm_sum1(base*taper)
```

Implement `mirror_refine(a0, time_limit, verbose=True)`:
```
def mirror_refine(a0, time_limit, top_m=120, verbose=True):
    a=norm_sum1(a0); n=len(a); rng=np.random.default_rng(123)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=fast_score(a); eta=1.0; stall=0; it=0
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic()-t_start < time_limit:
        it +=1
        c=fast_conv(a)
        maxc=c.max()
        if maxc <= best_score/(2*n)+1e-15: stall+=1 else? Actually best_score is total score, cmax_best=best_score/(2*n). Use compare.
        sel_m = min(top_m, max(20, n//100?) )
        idx = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:sel_m]
        # To include near peaks, maybe also ones > maxc - 0.05*maxc.
        thresh = maxc - 0.05*maxc
        extra = np.where(c > thresh)[0]
        unique = np.union1d(idx, extra)
        if len(unique)>300: unique = idx
        diff = c[unique] - maxc
        temp = max(1e-12, 0.05*maxc)
        w = np.exp(diff/temp); w/=w.sum()
        grad=np.zeros(n)
        for pos in range(len(unique)):
            k=int(unique[pos]); ww=float(w[pos])
            if ww<1e-12: continue
            js=max(0,k-n+1); je=min(n-1,k)
            idxs=np.arange(js,je+1)
            grad[js:je+1] += 2.0*ww * a[k-idxs]
        gmax=grad.max()
        if gmax<=0: continue
        base_eta=1.0/(2.0*gmax)  # since grad scale
        # try few etas
        candidates_eta = [base_eta*0.25, base_eta*0.5, base_eta, base_eta*2.0]
        best_trial_a=a; best_trial_s=fast_score(a)
        loga=np.log(a+1e-300)
        for eta_t in candidates_eta:
            newlog=loga - eta_t*grad
            # optional decay eta? add small entropy? no
            newlog-=newlog.max()
            an=np.exp(newlog); an=norm_sum1(an)
            sc=fast_score(an)
            if sc<best_trial_s:
                best_trial_s=sc; best_trial_a=an
        if best_trial_s < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score=best_trial_s; best_a=best_trial_a.copy(); stall=0
            # eta adaptation? keep
        else:
            stall += 1
        a=best_trial_a
        if it % 100 ==0 print...
        if stall > 2000:
            stall=0
            loga=np.log(best_a+1e-300)+0.05*rng.standard_normal(n)
            a=norm_sum1(np.exp(loga-loga.max()))
    return best_a, best_score
```

Now, code for candidate sweeps:
```
def run_candidate_swp(budget_seconds):
    t_start...
    top=[]
    lengths = ...
    for n in lengths:
       if time.monotonic()-t_start > budget: break
       # singular p
       for p in np.arange(0.0,2.2,0.1): consider(gen_singular_power(n,p))
       # raw power q
       for q in np.arange(0.0,1.0,0.07): consider(gen_raw_power(n,q))
       # beta
       vals=[0.0,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0,1.5,2.0,3.0]
       for al in vals:
          for be in vals:
             consider(gen_beta(n,al,be))
       # mixed
       for p in [0.3,0.6,0.9,1.2]:
          for lam in [0.2,0.4,0.6,0.8]:
             consider(gen_mixed(n,p,lam,finite='right_linear'))
       # random singular poly 50
       rng=np.random.default_rng(7)

Now code details for `fast_conv`:
```
def fast_conv(a):
    n = len(a)
    size = 2*n - 1
    if size <= 0: return np.array([], dtype=float)
    fs = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, fs)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A*A, fs)[:size]
    return c
```
For n=1, size=1, bit_length of 0? (size-1).bit_length() =0.bit_length()=0, 1<<0=1. Good. `a` float.

`fast_score` uses `c.max()`; if all zero returns 0. If sum<0.01? We normalize; if all zero, return inf.

return norm_sum1(a)
```

return norm_sum1(a)
```

`gen_random_shape(n, seed)`: Let's implement safe:
```
def gen_random_shape(n, seed):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    i=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
    x=i/(n-1) if n>1 else 0.0
    a=np.ones(n)
    if rng.random() < 0.8:
       a *= np.power(i+1.0, -rng.uniform(0.25,0.5))
    for deg in range(3):
       coeff = rng.uniform(-1.0,2.0)
       a *= (1.0 + coeff * np.power(x, deg+1))
    # if 50% add linear taper
    if rng.random() < 0.5:
       p = rng.uniform(0.2,1.5)
       a *= np.power(np.maximum(0.0,1.0-x), p)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<1e-8: return None
    return a/s
```
Maybe sometimes all zeros/neg; okay.

Now, final propose_candidate:
```
def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    t_global=time.monotonic()
    time_budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s)-30.0)
    top=[]
    # parent
    try:
       parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
       if parent.ndim==1 and parent.size>0 and parent.sum()>0:
           add_candidate(parent, top)
    except Exception:
       parent_n=6700
    else: parent_n=parent.size
    # lengths
    lengths=[1000,2000,4000,6000,8000,10000]
    if 200 <= parent_n <= 12000: lengths.append(parent_n)
    lengths=sorted(set([l for l in lengths if 500 <= l <= 12000]))
    t_cand_start=time.monotonic()
    cand_budget = min(time_budget*0.45, 600.0)
    for n in lengths:
       if time.monotonic()-t_cand_start > cand_budget: break
       # singular sweep
       for p in np.arange(0.0,2.1,0.1): add_candidate(gen_singular_power(n,p), top)
       # raw power
       for q in np.arange(0.0,1.0,0.07): add_candidate(gen_raw_power(n,q), top)
       # beta
       vals=[0.0,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0,1.5,2.0,3.0]
       for al in vals:
          for be in vals: add_candidate(gen_beta(n,al,be), top)
       # mixed
       for p in [0.3,0.6,0.9,1.2,1.6]:
          for lam in [0.2,0.4,0.6,0.8]: add_candidate(gen_mixed(n,p,lam), top)
       # random shapes 60
       for seed in range(60): add_candidate(gen_random_shape(n, 10000+n+seed), top)
    # maybe if time remains, do more random lengths? 
    remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic()-t_global)
    if remaining > 120:
       # refine best candidate
       top.sort(key=item_score)
       best_fast, best_n, best_a = top[0]
       refine_limit = min(remaining*0.55, 500.0)
       if refine_limit > 30:
           local_a, local_score = mirror_refine(best_a, refine_limit, top_m=120, verbose=True)
           if local_score < best_fast or True: add_candidate(local_a, top)
    remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic()-t_global)
    # true validation
    top.sort(key=item_score)
    max_true = 5 if remaining > 200 else 3
    best_true_score = float('inf')
    best_true_arr = None
    for i in range(min(max_true, len(top))):
       if time.monotonic()-t_global > time_budget-10: break
       fs,n,a = top[i]
       ts = true_score(a)
       if np.isfinite(ts):
           if ts < best_true_score:
               best_true_score=ts; best_true_arr=a
           print true score
    if best_true_arr is None:
       fallback = gen_singular_power(6700,1.0)
       best_true_arr = fallback
       best_true_score = true_score(fallback)
    # Ensure if true score of parent maybe not in top due fast? parent was added. Good.
    # If local candidate true not top due fast but true lower? We evaluate top by fast; could miss. But likely okay. Could also true_score second best after local.
    return [float(x) for x in np.asarray(best_true_arr,dtype=np.float64)]
```
Potential: If top[0] fast score is from p=0.5 length 10000 with fast 1.1, true maybe 1.15; another p=1 length 6700 fast 1.13 true 1.12. Evaluating top few handles.

    if a.size==0: return

```python
"""
Search for a low-score nonnegative sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a))/(sum(a))^2.

Strategy:
- Use fast FFT-based convolution for cheap internal scoring.
- Evaluate a broad suite of parametric families, especially endpoint-tapered
  power laws and beta-like densities, at several lengths.
- Keep a short list of the best candidates by fast score.
- Polish the best candidate with a mirror-descent / active-peak optimizer.
- Finally validate a few candidates with the real evaluate_sequence() and
  return the best truly-valid one.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def item_score(item):
    # Top-level key function for sorting candidate tuples.
    return item[0]


def norm_sum1(a):
    """Clean and normalize a sequence to nonnegative values with sum 1."""
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.ndim != 1:
        arr = arr.ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return arr.astype(np.float64)

    arr = arr.copy()
    bad = ~np.isfinite(arr)
    if np.any(bad):
        arr[bad] = 0.0
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)

    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        arr = np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
        return arr / float(arr.size)
    return arr / s


def fast_conv(a):
    """Fast linear convolution a*a via FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    fft_size = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, fft_size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, fft_size)[:size]
    return c


def fast_score(a):
    """Fast proxy for evaluate_sequence, after normalizing to sum 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = norm_sum1(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    c = fast_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def fast_score_sum1(a):
    """Fast score for an already-sum-to-1 array."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = fast_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def true_score(a):
    """Exact score using the provided evaluate_sequence."""
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        arr = norm_sum1(arr)
        lst = [float(x) for x in arr]
        val = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
        return val
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def gen_power_taper(n, q, p):
    """
    a_i ~ (i+1)^(-q) * (1 - (i+0.5)/n)^p.
    This includes pure powers (p=0), linear tapers (q=0, p=1), and the
    very useful.sqrt(taper) family (q=0.5, p~0.5-1.5).
    """
    n = int(n)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = (i + 0.5) / float(n)
    base = np.power(i + 1.0, -float(q))
    if p == 0.0:
        taper = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        taper = np.power(np.maximum(0.0, 1.0 - x), float(p))
    a = base * taper
    return norm_sum1(a)


def gen_beta(n, alpha, beta):
    """
    Finite beta-like family:
        a_i ~ x^alpha (1-x)^beta, x=(i+0.5)/n.
    """
    n = int(n)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = (i + 0.5) / float(n)
    xn = np.maximum(x, 1.0 - n)

    if alpha == 0.0:
        ax = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        ax = np.power(x, float(alpha))

    if beta == 0.0:
        bx = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        bx = np.power(1.0 - x, float(beta))

    a = ax * bx
    return norm_sum1(a)


def gen_gaussian(n, center, width):
    """A simple bump family; not expected to dominate, but helps diversity."""
    n = int(n)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = (i + 0.5) / float(n)
    a = np.exp(-((x - float(center)) ** 2) / (2.0 * float(width) ** 2))
    return norm_sum1(a)


def gen_random_shape(n, seed):
    """
    Random positive shape:
      power-taper times a low-degree polynomial, clipped nonnegative.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    n = int(n)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = (i + 0.5) / float(n)

    q = float(rng.uniform(-0.1, 0.7))
    p = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0))
    a = np.power(i + 1.0, -q)
    if p > 0:
        a *= np.power(np.maximum(0.0, 1.0 - x), p)

    for deg in range(3):
        coeff = float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 2.0))
        factor = 1.0 + coeff * (x ** (deg + 1))
        a *= factor

    if rng.random() < 0.35:
        # Add a small positive bump in the interior.
        c = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 0.75))
        w = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.25))
        bump = np.exp(-((x - c) ** 2) / (2.0 * w * w))
        a += 0.3 * bump

    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-8:
        return None
    return a / s


def add_candidate(arr, top, max_keep=30):
    """Normalize, fast-score, and insert into the top candidate list."""
    if arr is None:
        return
    a = norm_sum1(arr)
    if a.size == 0:
        return
    sc = fast_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return
    top.append((sc, int(a.size), a))
    if len(top) > max_keep:
        top.sort(key=item_score)
        top.pop()


def active_gradient(a, c, top_m=100):
    """
    Compute a gradient/subgradient from the largest convolution peaks.
    For F(a)=max_k (a*a)_k and soft active set weights p_k,
    grad_i = 2 * sum_k p_k * a_{k-i}.
    """
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)

    if c.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    maxc = float(np.max(c))
    if maxc <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if n <= top_m:
        idx = np.arange(n)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(c, -top_m)[-top_m:]

    # Include near-max peaks if there are not too many.
    thresh = maxc - 0.10 * maxc
    near = np.where(c > thresh)[0]
    if near.size > 0 and near.size < 3 * top_m:
        idx = np.unique(np.concatenate((idx, near)))

    diff = c[idx] - maxc
    temp = max(1e-15, 0.05 * maxc)
    w = np.exp(np.clip(diff / temp, -50.0, 0.0))
    ws = float(w.sum())
    if ws <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
        w = np.ones(idx.size, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, idx.size)
    else:
        w = w / ws

    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(idx.size):
        k = int(idx[j])
        ww = float(w[j])
        if ww < 1e-14:
            continue

        js = k - n + 1
        if js < 0:
            js = 0
        je = k
        if je >= n:
            je = n - 1
        if js > je:
            continue

        # partner indices are k - je, ..., k - js
        seg = a[k - je:k - js + 1][::-1]
        grad[js:je + 1] += 2.0 * ww * seg

    return grad


def mirror_refine(a0, time_limit, top_m=110, verbose=True):
    """
    Mirror descent / multiplicative weights on the active peak objective.
    Returns (best_sequence, best_fast_score).
    """
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return a, float("inf")

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = fast_score_sum1(a)
    if verbose:
        print("  mirror start score: " + repr(best_score) + " n=" + str(n))

    stall = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < max(0.0, time_limit):
        it += 1
        if it > 2000000:
            break

        c = fast_conv(a)
        current_score = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(c))

        grad = active_gradient(a, c, top_m=top_m)
        gmax = float(np.max(grad))
        if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax <= 1e-30:
            break

        loga = np.log(a + 1e-300)
        base_eta = 30.0 / gmax
        if not np.isfinite(base_eta) or base_eta <= 0.0:
            base_eta = 1.0
        base_eta = min(base_eta, 1e8)

        trial_best_score = current_score
        trial_best_a = a

        for factor in (0.35, 0.85, 1.7):
            newlog = loga - (base_eta * factor) * grad
            newlog -= float(np.max(newlog))
            an = np.exp(newlog)
            an = np.maximum(an, 0.0)
            ss = float(an.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss < 1e-12:
                continue
            an = an / ss
            sc = fast_score_sum1(an)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < trial_best_score:
                trial_best_score = sc
                trial_best_a = an

        if trial_best_score < best_score - 1e-13:
            best_score = trial_best_score
            best_a = trial_best_a.copy()
            stall = 0
            if verbose and it % 1000 == 0:
                print("  mirror t=" + str(it) + " score=" + repr(best_score))
        else:
            stall += 1

        # Accept only if not worse than current, to avoid drift.
        if trial_best_score <= current_score + 1e-14:
            a = trial_best_a
        else:
            # Increase step size if no trial improved even locally.
            base_eta *= 1.25

        if verbose and it % 2000 == 0:
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
            print("  mirror iter=" + str(it) +
                  " (" + format(elapsed, ".1f") + "s) current=" +
                  repr(current_score) + " best=" + repr(best_score))

        if stall > 1800:
            stall = 0
            rebase = best_a + 1e-15
            log_rebase = np.log(rebase)
            noise = 0.08 * rng.standard_normal(n)
            loga = log_rebase + noise
            loga -= float(np.max(loga))
            a = np.exp(loga)
            a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
            ss = float(a.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss < 1e-12:
                a = best_a.copy()
            else:
                a = a / ss

    return best_a, best_score


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    """
    Main entry point. Returns a Python list of nonnegative floats.
    """
    t_global = time.monotonic()
    total_time = max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 25.0)

    top = []

    # Load parent if available and add as a candidate.
    parent_n = 6700
    try:
        hp = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if hp.ndim == 1 and hp.size > 0:
            parent_n = int(hp.size)
            add_candidate(hp, top)
            if hp.size > 0:
                print("Parent included: n=" + str(parent_n))
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Candidate lengths. Bias toward the parent length but allow larger.
    lengths = [800, 1500, 2500, 4000, 5500, 6700, 8000, 10000]
    if 500 <= parent_n <= 12000:
        lengths.append(parent_n)
    lengths = sorted(set([int(x) for x in lengths if 500 <= int(x) <= 12000]))

    print("Candidate lengths: " + str(lengths))

    # ---------------- Phase 1: parametric sweeps ----------------
    sweep_budget = min(total_time * 0.35, 500.0)
    t_sweep = time.monotonic()

    q_vals = [0.0, 0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.4, 0.45, 0.5, 0.55, 0.65]
    p_vals = [0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.25, 1.5, 2.0]

    beta_vals = [0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0]

    gauss_centers = [0.25, 0.35, 0.50, 0.65, 0.75]
    gauss_widths = [0.05, 0.10, 0.18, 0.30]

    seed_base = 9001
    for n in lengths:
        if time.monotonic() - t_sweep > sweep_budget:
            break

        # Core power-taper family.
        for q in q_vals:
            for p in p_vals:
                add_candidate(gen_power_taper(n, q, p), top)

        # Finite beta family.
        for al in beta_vals:
            for be in beta_vals:
                add_candidate(gen_beta(n, al, be), top)

        # A few Gaussians for diversity.
        for gc in gauss_centers:
            for gw in gauss_widths:
                add_candidate(gen_gaussian(n, gc, gw), top)

        # Random shapes.
        for kk in range(70):
            add_candidate(gen_random_shape(n, seed_base + n + kk), top)

        if time.monotonic() - t_sweep > sweep_budget:
            break

    print("Sweep done. Top fast score: " + repr(top[0][0]) if len(top) else "No candidates")

    # Sort top candidates by fast score.
    top.sort(key=item_score)

    # ---------------- Phase 2: local refinement ----------------
    remaining = total_time - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    local_best_relative = None
    local_best_score = float("inf")

    if remaining > 120 and len(top) > 0:
        # Refine the overall best, and also the best with n <= 8000.
        refine_targets = []
        seen_n = set()

        for fs, nn, aa in top[:5]:
            refine_targets.append(aa)

        for fs, nn, aa in top:
            if nn <= 8000 and nn not in seen_n:
                refine_targets.append(aa)
                seen_n.add(nn)
                if len(refine_targets) >= 4:
                    break

        # Deduplicate by length and approximate first score.
        unique_targets = []
        used = set()
        for tt in refine_targets:
            key = (int(tt.size), float(tt[0]))
            if key not in used:
                used.add(key)
                unique_targets.append(tt)
            if len(unique_targets) >= 3:
                break

        for target in unique_targets:
            remaining = total_time - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
            if remaining < 45:
                break
            local_budget = min(remaining * 0.35, 420.0)
            if local_budget <= 20:
                continue
            print("Refining target n=" + str(target.size) +
                  " fast score=" + repr(fast_score(target)))
            la, lsc = mirror_refine(target, local_budget, top_m=110, verbose=True)
            if np.isfinite(lsc):
                add_candidate(la, top)
                if lsc < local_best_score:
                    local_best_score = lsc
                    local_best_relative = la

    # ---------------- Phase 3: exact validation of best candidates ----------------
    top.sort(key=item_score)
    remaining = total_time - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if remaining > 60:
        max_true = 8 if remaining > 220 else 4
    else:
        max_true = 1

    best_true_score = float("inf")
    best_true_arr = None

    checked = 0
    for item in top:
        if checked >= max_true:
            break
        if time.monotonic() - t_global > total_time - 5:
            break
        fs, nn, aa = item
        ts = true_score(aa)
        checked += 1
        if np.isfinite(ts):
            print("  true validation: fast=" + repr(fs) +
                  " true=" + repr(ts) + " n=" + str(nn))
            if ts < best_true_score:
                best_true_score = ts
                best_true_arr = aa
        else:
            print("  true validation failed for n=" + str(nn))

    # Also validate the local refinement result directly if it is not already
    # among the exact-validated top candidates.
    if local_best_relative is not None:
        ts = true_score(local_best_relative)
        if np.isfinite(ts):
            print("  local true score=" + repr(ts))
            if ts < best_true_score:
                best_true_score = ts
                best_true_arr = local_best_relative

    # Fallback: a very good deterministic family.
    if best_true_arr is None:
        n_fb = parent_n if 500 <= parent_n <= 12000 else 8000
        best_true_arr = gen_power_taper(int(n_fb), 0.5, 1.0)
        best_true_score = true_score(best_true_arr)
        if not np.isfinite(best_true_score):
            # ultimate fallback
            best_true_arr = np.ones(1000, dtype=np.float64)
            best_true_arr = norm_sum1(best_true_arr)

    final = norm_sum1(best_true_arr)
    final = np.asarray(final, dtype=np.float64)
    final = np.clip(final, 0.0, None)
    return [float(x) for x in final]
```
